- **pinecone-403-this-project-already-cont-b763e4ed5d638e3c**: Each project is capped at 20 serverless indexes. The error message says exactly what to do: delete unused indexes or use a new project. Namespaces are the intended escape hatch. Not the full reference manual. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/pinecone-403-this-project-already-cont-b763e4ed5d638e3c))
- **pinecone-404-index-not-found-usually-m-65ab55d2b1b79b42**: A 404 on an index that should exist is most often the API key of a different project, or a name with a case or dash error. Verify project, then name, then existence. Not for different error messages. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/pinecone-404-index-not-found-usually-m-65ab55d2b1b79b42))
- **pinecone-409-index-already-exists-make-0cca2581dd7022ea**: 409 Already Exists means the name is taken. Check-then-create races; use try/except around create or treat 409 as success when the existing index matches your spec. Not the full reference manual. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/pinecone-409-index-already-exists-make-0cca2581dd7022ea))
- **pinecone-429-rate-limited-back-off-wit-632a76593831d3cb**: 429 Too Many Requests means slow down. Retry with exponential backoff and jitter, batch upserts to cut request count, and watch metrics before you hit the wall. Not the full reference manual. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/pinecone-429-rate-limited-back-off-wit-632a76593831d3cb))
- **pinecone-5xx-transient-server-errors-r-9db4f74721c8d04a**: 500, 502, 503, and 504 are Pinecone-side and usually transient. Retry with exponential backoff and jitter. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/pinecone-5xx-transient-server-errors-r-9db4f74721c8d04a))

- **pinecone-diagnosis-401s-start-after-an-057d3b72bacc6bf4**: Symptom: everything 401s right after rotating a key. Some deployment still holds the old key; inventory every consumer and update them together. Not the full reference manual. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/pinecone-diagnosis-401s-start-after-an-057d3b72bacc6bf4))
- **pinecone-diagnosis-delete-by-filter-re-733a6e6b1be874dd**: Symptom: delete with a metadata filter reports success but records remain. Check the namespace, the filter match, and whether deletion protection or the wrong index is involved. Not the full reference manual. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/pinecone-diagnosis-delete-by-filter-re-733a6e6b1be874dd))
- **pinecone-diagnosis-embedding-values-lo-4bcf7d7fd2b80478**: Symptom: fetched vectors differ slightly from what you upserted. This is float32 rounding on write, expected and harmless; only investigate if the drift is large. Not the full reference manual. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/pinecone-diagnosis-embedding-values-lo-4bcf7d7fd2b80478))
- **pinecone-diagnosis-hybrid-dense-plus-s-fd0341e028ffe251**: Symptom: hybrid queries return worse results than dense-only. Check that sparse values are present on records, the alpha balance is sane, and you picked the right combination pattern. Not the full reference manual. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/pinecone-diagnosis-hybrid-dense-plus-s-fd0341e028ffe251))
- **pinecone-diagnosis-index-not-ready-rig-80fb4635789ec919**: Symptom: operations fail on a just-created index. Creation is async; poll describe_index until status.ready is true before writing or querying. Not the full reference manual. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/pinecone-diagnosis-index-not-ready-rig-80fb4635789ec919))
- **pinecone-diagnosis-just-upserted-vecto-9c5ed0e81b624712**: Symptom: upsert returns success but immediate queries miss the new vectors. Usually read-after-write timing or a namespace slip; confirm with fetch, then adjust the read path. Not the full reference manual. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/pinecone-diagnosis-just-upserted-vecto-9c5ed0e81b624712))
- **pinecone-diagnosis-langchain-vector-st-7737771bcaaa49f2**: Symptom: PineconeVectorStore construction succeeds but the first operation fails. LangChain never creates the index; create it first with matching dimension. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/pinecone-diagnosis-langchain-vector-st-7737771bcaaa49f2))
- **pinecone-diagnosis-metadata-filter-mat-3ad310325ebbe1fe**: Symptom: query with a filter returns empty but unfiltered query works. Check field names, value types, and operator shapes; one wrong type poisons the whole filter. Not the full reference manual. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/pinecone-diagnosis-metadata-filter-mat-3ad310325ebbe1fe))
- **pinecone-diagnosis-no-way-to-return-ev-f41598061f12c246**: Symptom: you need all vectors and query cannot do it. By design there is no select-all; page through list plus fetch in batches, or take a backup for a static copy. Not the full reference manual. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/pinecone-diagnosis-no-way-to-return-ev-f41598061f12c246))

- **pinecone-diagnosis-recall-worse-than-e-8db1bc18787b1eb4**: Symptom: queries miss results you expect. - **pinecone-diagnosis-upsert-rejected-vec-4b3bd72907694f05**: Symptom: upsert fails saying the vector dimension does not match the index. - **pinecone-dimension-and-metric-must-mat-bdff4cbb25324d34**: Dimension must equal the embedding model's output width and metric must suit the embedding type.
